Upstream

Dockerfile

# STEP1: CLONE THE CODE
FROM alpine/git as builder_prepare
WORKDIR /home/
ARG GIT_REPO=https://github.com/mickael-kerjean/filestash
ARG GIT_BRANCH=master
RUN git clone --depth 1 --single-branch --branch ${GIT_BRANCH} ${GIT_REPO}

# STEP2: BUILD BACKEND
FROM golang:1.26-trixie AS builder_backend
WORKDIR /home/filestash/
COPY --from=builder_prepare /home/filestash/ .
RUN apt-get update > /dev/null && \
    apt-get install -y curl make > /dev/null 2>&1 && \
    apt-get install -y libjpeg-dev libtiff-dev libpng-dev libwebp-dev libraw-dev libheif-dev libgif-dev libvips-dev > /dev/null 2>&1 && \
    apt-get install -y libavcodec-dev libavdevice-dev libavfilter-dev libavformat-dev libswresample-dev libswscale-dev libavutil-dev > /dev/null 2>&1 && \
    make init && \
    make build && \
    mkdir -p ./dist/data/state/config/ && \
    cp config/config.json ./dist/data/state/config/config.json

# STEP3: BUILD PROD IMAGE
FROM debian:stable-slim
MAINTAINER mickael@kerjean.me
WORKDIR /app/
COPY --from=builder_backend /home/filestash/dist/ .
RUN apt-get update > /dev/null && \
    apt-get -y upgrade > /dev/null && \
    apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends ca-certificates curl ffmpeg libbrotli1 poppler-utils && \
    useradd filestash && \
    chown -R filestash:filestash /app/ && \
    find /app/data/ -type d -exec chmod 770 {} \; && \
    find /app/data/ -type f -exec chmod 760 {} \; && \
    chmod 730 /app/filestash && \
    rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/* && \
    rm -rf /tmp/*

USER filestash
CMD ["/app/filestash"]
EXPOSE 8334

FROM github:NixOS/nixpkgs/nixos-unstable AS pkgs
FROM . AS src

BUILDER backend
  IMPORT ${pkgs.bash} ${pkgs.coreutils} ${pkgs.go_1_26} ${pkgs.git} ${pkgs.curl} ${pkgs.cacert} ${pkgs.pkg-config} ${pkgs.gcc} \
      ${pkgs.brotli} ${pkgs.libraw} ${pkgs.ffmpeg} ${pkgs.libjpeg} ${pkgs.libtiff} \
      ${pkgs.libpng} ${pkgs.libwebp} ${pkgs.libheif} ${pkgs.giflib} ${pkgs.vips} \
      ${pkgs.zlib} ${pkgs.lcms2} ${pkgs.libyuv} ${pkgs.glib}
  IMPORT ${pkgs.brotli.dev} ${pkgs.libraw.dev} ${pkgs.ffmpeg.dev}
  ENV GOMODCACHE=$PWD/gomodcache
  FETCH \
      mkdir -p gomodcache && \
      curl --fail --location https://raw.githubusercontent.com/mickael-kerjean/filestash/master/go.mod --output go.mod && \
      curl --fail --location https://raw.githubusercontent.com/mickael-kerjean/filestash/master/go.sum --output go.sum && \
      GOMODCACHE=$PWD/gomodcache go mod download all && \
      curl --fail --location https://raw.githubusercontent.com/nothings/stb/5736b15f7ea0ffb08dd38af21067c314d6a3aae9/stb_image.h --output image_psd_vendor.h && \
      curl --fail --location https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/xterm/3.12.2/xterm.js --output xterm.js && \
      curl --fail --location https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/xterm/3.12.2/addons/fit/fit.js --output fit.js && \
      curl --fail --location https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/xterm/3.12.2/xterm.css --output xterm.css
  COPY ${src}/context/ .
  RUN <<BUILD
set -e
mkdir -p server/plugin/plg_image_c server/plugin/plg_handler_console/src server/plugin/plg_widget_console/assets/vendor
cp image_psd_vendor.h server/plugin/plg_image_c/image_psd_vendor.h
cat xterm.js fit.js > server/plugin/plg_handler_console/src/xterm.js
cp xterm.css server/plugin/plg_handler_console/src/xterm.css
cat xterm.js fit.js > server/plugin/plg_widget_console/assets/vendor/xterm.js
cp xterm.css server/plugin/plg_widget_console/assets/vendor/xterm.css
(cd server/common && go run ../generator/mime.go && go run ../generator/constants.go)
mkdir -p dist/data/state/config
go build --tags fts5 -o dist/filestash cmd/main.go
cp config/config.json dist/data/state/config/config.json
BUILD

SERVICE filestash
  IMPORT ${pkgs.glibc} ${pkgs.cacert} ${pkgs.curl} ${pkgs.ffmpeg} ${pkgs.poppler-utils} ${pkgs.brotli}
  COPY ${backend}/dist /app
  START /app/filestash
  PORT http = 8334
  STATEDIR /app/data
  CACHEDIR /app/cache
  CLAIM egress

check.sh 
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
mode=${1:?usage: ./check.sh docker|cix}; root=$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "$0")" && pwd)
cix=${CIX:-"$root/../../../../target/debug/cix"}; name=migrate-r5-filestash; container=; unit=
cleanup() { [[ -z $unit ]] || sudo -n systemctl stop "$unit" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true; [[ -z $container ]] || docker rm -f "$container" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true; }
trap cleanup EXIT
probe() { for _ in $(seq 1 120); do curl --fail --silent --max-time 2 --dump-header - --output /dev/null http://127.0.0.1:18094/ 2>/dev/null | grep -qi '^X-Powered-By: Filestash' && return 0; sleep 1; done; return 1; }
[[ -d $root/context ]] || { echo 'context/ missing — run ../../fetch.sh filestash first' >&2; exit 1; }
cd "$root"
case $mode in
  docker) image=$(timeout 1200 docker build --quiet --file Dockerfile --tag "$name" .); printf 'docker image %s\n' "$image"; container=$(docker run --detach --rm --publish 127.0.0.1:18094:8334 "$name"); probe; printf 'PASS docker\n' ;;
  cix) item=$(timeout 1200 "$cix" build .#filestash); printf 'cix item %s\n' "$item"; unit=$(sudo -n "$cix" run --detach "$item" | tail -n1); printf 'cix unit %s\n' "$unit"; probe; printf 'PASS cix\n' ;;
  *) echo 'usage: ./check.sh docker|cix' >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
